Brother KH-930  —  built-in stitch-pattern atlas
==================================================

Source          : LH531212-4bit.bin  (Sharp LH53512, 64K x 8, 4-bit output mode)
ROM map         : 0x0000..0x10D0 descriptor table (615 x 7-byte records)
                  0x10D1..0xF701 pattern data (row-codes + 1bpp bitmaps)
                  0xF702..0xFFFF zero padding
Record layout   : [0]BCD-index [1]type [2]BCD-width
                  [3]height-units(<<4) [4]BCD-height-tens [5..6]offset(LE)
                  catalogue = type*100 + BCD(index);  H = BCD(b4)*10 + (b3>>4)
Data layout     : optional ceil(H/2) row-code nibbles (1 per row), then
                  row-major 1 bpp bitmap, stride = ceil(W/8), bit 0 = leftmost.
Row-code nibble : omitted when all-zero (plain knit, no carriage/colour change).
                  Present otherwise: 0 = knit/lace-transfer, 2 = knit carriage,
                  1..4 = colour / operation rows.
Each preview row is prefixed with its row-code value (e.g. " 2|....").

Library: 615 patterns.

CATEGORY SUMMARY
----------------
category                 count    catalog rowcodes
fairisle / single motif     99       1-99     67/99 
multicolor                 100    100-199     48/100
lace                       100    200-299     57/100
tuck / skip                100    300-399     63/100
weaving / thread lace      100    400-499     54/100
garter                      56    500-555     45/56 
extras - glyphs             53    601-653      7/53 
extras - logo/text           3    701-703      0/3  
test patterns                4    881-884      2/4
